sVar1 = DAT_UnitsState.units[(int)unitID].y;
sVar3 = DAT_UnitsState.units[(int)unitID].x;
}

DAT_UnitsState.units[(int)unitID].field300_0x3f8 = 0;
}
LAB_00575c13:
if (0 < DAT_UnitsState.units[(int)unitID].animationFrame) {
return;
}
DAT_UnitsState.units[(int)unitID].animationCycleNumber = 0;
LAB_00575c21:
if (DAT_UnitsState.units[(int)unitID].state.generic == US_RELOAD_WEAPONUnk) {
DAT_UnitsState.units[(int)unitID].state.generic = US_DETERMINE_NEXT_STATEUnk;
}
DAT_UnitsState.units[(int)unitID].horseArcherShootingVariation = 0;
return;
}
